Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-8..-6, 3..5)
find y: int(-3, -1, 2)
find z: int(-2, 0, 2)
find small_xy: bool

such that

sum([sum([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) >= -4,
(small_xy) <-> (sum([product([3,x;int(1..)]),product([2,y;int(1..)]);int(1..)]) <= 0)

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (z#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (!(z#sign_magnitude_3)) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (z#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]))
  or([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-2, 2]) = -2,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-2, 2]) = 0,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-2, 2]) = 2;int(1..)])
